ys.min() + 1\n    w = xs.max() - xs.min() + 1\n    elongation = max(h,w) / min(h,w)\n    fill_ratio = size / (h * w)\n    # Void: elongation < 2.0 AND fill_ratio > 0.4 (roundish)\n    # Crack: elongation >= 2.0 OR (large size with low fill ratio)\n    is_void = (elongation < 2.0) and (fill_ratio > 0.4)\n    if is_void:\n        void_pixels += size\n    print(f\"hole {i}: size={size}, {h}x{w}, elong={elongation:.1f}, fill={fill_ratio:.2f} -> {'VOID' if is_void else 'crack/other'}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal void pixels: {void_pixels}\")\nprint(f\"Part pixels (filled): {part_filled.sum()}\")\nvoid_ratio_pct = 100 * void_pixels / part_filled.sum()\nprint(f\"Void ratio: {void_ratio_pct:.4f}%\")\n", "out": "hole 1: size=199, 28x23, elong=1.2, fill=0.31 -> crack/other\nhole 2: size=4, 3x2, elong=1.5, fill=0.67 -> VOID\nhole 3: size=1, 1x1, elong=1.0, fill=1.00 -> VOID\nhole 4: size=2, 1x2, elong=2.0, fill=1.00 -> crack/other\nhole 5: size=4, 2x3, elong=1.5, fill=0.67 -> VOID\nhole 6: size=2, 1x2, elong=2.0, fill=1.00 -> crack/other\nhole 7: size=3, 2x2, elong=1.0, fill=0.75 -> VOID\nhole 8: size=5, 2x3, elong=1.5, fill=0.83 -> VOID\nhole 9: size=1, 1x1, elong=1.0, fill=1.00 -> VOID\nhole 10: size=1, 1x1, elong=1.0, fill=1.00 -> VOID\nhole 11: size=4, 3x2, elong=1.5, fill=0.67 -> VOID\nhole 12: size=10, 5x4, elong=1.2, fill=0.50 -> VOID\nhole 13: size=1, 1x1, elong=1.0, fill=1.00 -> VOID\nhole 14: size=9, 3x5", "err": "", "pics": []}, {"step": 8, "purpose": "Purpose: Verify the classification by checking if the small \"voids\" are real or just noise, and double-check hole 1 is indeed a crack.", "code": "# Let's verify: the small holes (1-10 px) -     document.getElementById("tv-head").textContent = e.image + "  |  condition " + cs.value;

    document.getElementById("tv-code").textContent = c.code || "(no code block in the reply)";
    document.getElementById("tv-out").textContent = c.out;
    document.getElementById("tv-err").textContent = c.err;

    var last = k === r.cells.length - 1;
    document.getElementById("tv-end").textContent = last ? ("End of episode. Agent number: " + (r.agent === null ? "none" : r.agent + " %") + "  |  ground truth: " + e.gt + " %  |  verdict: " + r.status + " (" + r.reason + "), truth would be " + e.gt_status) : "";
  }
  sel.onchange = function(){ i = +sel.value; k = 0; show(); };
  cs.onchange = function(){ k = 0; show(); };
  document.getElementById("tv-prev").onclick = function(){ k = Math.max(0, k-1); show(); };
  document.getElementById("tv-next").onclick = function(){ k = Math.min(E[i].runs[cs.value].cells.length-1, k+1); show(); };
  show();
